# Miss analysis on the DEV splits (n=200 set and 143-call set)

**Scope.** DEV halves only: 100 of the 200 calls in `data/coding_n200.csv` (`data/split_n200.csv`) and 70 of the 143 calls in `data/coding.csv` (`data/split_n143.csv`). Both splits use seed 20261031 and are stratified on conceptual × applied × (critical or governance ≥ 1). TEST calls were not read. Runs examined: on the 200 set, prompt v4 few-shot×10 12B (`v4fs`), v4 zero-shot 12B (`v4zs`) and v3 zero-shot 12B (`v3zs`). On the 143 set, v4 zero-shot 12B (`v4zs`) and v3 vote5 12B (`v3v5`). The frame is the rubric text in `src/prompts.py` (v3/v4).

## DEV distributions (% of calls at 0 / 1 / 2)

| Applied | 16 / 48 / 36 | 14 / 49 / 37 | 7 / 61 / 31 | 8 / 58 / 34 |
| Governance | 85 / 12 / 3 | 86 / 13 / 1 | 73 / 21 / 6 | 67 / 30 / 3 |

Pooled DEV (170 calls), used for the v5 base-rate note: conceptual 7 / 59 / 34, applied 12 / 54 / 34, critical 72 / 24 / 4, governance 80 / 16 / 4.

## Patterns

### Conceptual, human 2 vs 1 (largest miss on the 200 set)
- **The model withholds 2 when content is developed but not mechanistic.** 200 set: human 2 scored 1 on 32 calls (v4zs) and 23 (v4fs); 25 and 16 of these have 6 or more AI sentences. The humans give 2 when an excerpt develops specific AI content across several statements, even without a mechanism. Examples:
  - several capabilities described with what they do: S2-0016, "leverage generative AI to scrape reviews to answer traveler questions about amenities"; S2-0064, "an ambient AI medical scribe … detecting things like disease markers earlier";
  - AI's technical requirements with reasons: S2-0065, batteries going "to more than 6,000 milliampere-hour … due to AI"; S2-0039, AI data centers "require continuous and substantial power";
  - distinctions among model types or sources: S2-0096, internal data science against "external LLM models depending … for the natural language search".
- **The v4 wording pushes toward 1.** "This is the common sheet score. Between 1 and 2, assign 1" and "a chain rather than a mention" are applied literally. Model notes often say "mentions X" and stop at 1.
- **Length tracks the human score strongly on the 200 set** (DEV): conceptual 2 for 0 of 38 excerpts with 1–2 sentences, 6 of 17 with 4–5, 11 of 12 with 6–8, 8 of 11 with 9–15, and 13 of 13 with 16 or more.
- **On the 143 set the threshold is much higher.** Conceptual 2 for 0 of 10 excerpts with 6–8 sentences, 4 of 16 with 9–15, and 15 of 18 with 16 or more. The model's misses there run both ways (2→1: 5, 1→2: 5). See "Suspected inconsistencies" below.

### Conceptual, human 1 vs 0
- **The model gives 0 to brief real mentions that humans code 1:** 200 set, 8–10 calls, nearly all with 1–3 sentences. Examples:
  - AI as a market driver: S2-0060, "The market is driven by 2 engines: one is the cloud, second is the AI"; S2-0114, power demand of AI data centers;
  - AI-powered platform labels: S2-0128;
  - plans for commercial AI products: S2-0061.
  The v4 rule ("pure hype … is 0", "AI as market backdrop") leads the model to 0. The humans reserve 0 for AI only named in a question or as a bare label.
- **The model gives 1 where humans gave 0:** 6–7 calls, all S3 IDs (S3-0001, S3-0006, S3-0029, S3-0050 …). These look like a coding-batch effect rather than a rule the model can learn (see below).

### Applied, human 2 vs 1
- **v4 under-scores 2:** 200 set, 21 calls (v4zs) and 13 (v4fs); 17 and 10 have 6 or more sentences. Humans give 2 when two or more uses are described with what the AI does, for example:
  - S2-0009: "credit memo narratives, which leverages generative AI to automate the creation of a credit memo" plus pricing;
  - S2-0020: "MLR Bot, which is for the content approval process" plus CRM Bot and voice control;
  - S2-0112: "call summarization, sentiment analysis, real-time knowledge assistance".
- **v3 fits the 200 set better on 2s but over-scores on the 143 set.** On the 200 set v3 gives human 2 → 1 on 8 calls and human 1 → 2 on 10. On the 143 set (v3 vote5), human 1 → 2 rises to 22 calls, against 1 for v4. The 143-set coders kept a list of uses at 1 (TR029: three uses each with a function, applied 1; TR034, TR048, TR059 likewise).
- **Measured outcomes are not always 2 for humans.** S2-0092 ("more than 1 million messages in Q2, up 108%") and S2-0121 ("1 million chats") are human 1, while S2-0056 ("1 million interactions") is human 2.

### Applied, human 1 vs 0
- **The model scores 0 when the company supplies AI demand:** 200 set, 8–11 calls (S2-0006 tools for packaging AI processors, S2-0065 batteries for AI phones, S2-0039 power for AI data centers, S2-0123 AI sales). Humans code 1. The v4 rule "AI as market backdrop, demand, or hype is 0" causes this. Humans keep 0 for AI mentioned only as someone else's trend (S2-0113, S2-0114, S2-0129 human 0).

### Critical, human 1 vs 0
- **The model misses short risk remarks:** 200 set, 13–14 calls scored 0 where humans gave ≥1 (v4zs, v3zs); 7 of these also have human governance ≥1. Humans code critical 1 for:
  - AI-enabled threats: S2-0029, "an uptick in the use of AI-based attacks";
  - fraud "menace" handled by AI: S2-0008;
  - human judgment kept in the loop: S2-0043, "an enabler to human judgment";
  - an explicit risk remark: S2-0012, "you don't have to be concerned about risk";
  - AI disruption questions: S2-0036, S2-0158.
  On the 200 set governance ≥1 comes with critical ≥1 on 22 of 29 calls; on the 143 set, 19 of 43.
- **The model over-scores critical on remarks that are not about AI's limits:** 200 set 4–9 calls, 143 set 6–7. Examples: S2-0089, "leaders … say AI is really overwhelming"; S2-0063, "huge opportunities there for AI to spot problems"; S2-0130. On the 143 set: TR007, TR044, TR053, TR092, TR163, TR214.

### Governance, human 1 vs 0
- **v3/v4 rules mostly match.** Misses are 14–15 on the 200 set and 9–10 on the 143 set, split both ways.
- **Model 0, human 1:** AI security or safety businesses (S2-0029 cybersecurity platform, S2-0110 SentinelOne partnership, TR057 machine-learning anti-bot, TR220 perimeter security). Also S2-0036 (an analyst question; see below).
- **Model 1, human 0:** "human-in-the-loop" and guardrail mentions (S2-0097, S2-0112), and on the 143 set "enterprise governance requirements" (TR005) and "inside our firewall" (TR071). The v3 rule already says these passing phrases are 0, but the model does not follow it.


## Suspected inconsistencies in the human codes (for the advisor; no codes changed)

1. **Batch effect inside the 200 set (S3 IDs against S2 IDs).** Over all 200 calls, among excerpts with 1–3 AI sentences, conceptual 0 is 62% for S3 IDs (18 of 29) against 2% for S2 IDs (1 of 65). No S3 call has conceptual 2, against 50% of S2 calls. Comparable short excerpts get different codes, for example:
   - S3-0001, "AI-powered marketplace", conceptual 0, applied 1, against S2-0128, "AI-powered platform", conceptual 1;
   - S3-0006, "AI for scheduling", conceptual 0, applied 1, against S2-0075, "AI-based SOP workflow management", conceptual 1, applied 1.
   S3 calls also have applied 1 with conceptual 0 (S3-0001, S3-0006, S3-0007, S3-0016, S3-0026, S3-0029, S3-0050, S3-0051), which the rubric makes unlikely: a stated AI use usually implies at least brief AI content. Were the S3 calls coded by a different coder or round?
2. **Conceptual and applied 2 threshold differs between the two coding sets.** The 143 set gives conceptual 1 to every DEV excerpt with 6–8 AI sentences (0 of 10 at 2) and 2 to 4 of 16 with 9–15. The 200 set gives 2 to 11 of 12 and 8 of 11. Excerpts of similar substance:
   - 143 set, conceptual 1: TR059 (GPT for content, machine learning on workflows, robotic process automation, compared with competitors' "superficial" use) and TR034 (Okta AI risk scoring and natural-language log questions);
   - 200 set, conceptual 2: S2-0064 (predictive analytics, ambient scribe, diagnostics) and S2-0016 (Expedia generative AI tools).
   Applied shows the same split: TR029 (three uses, each with a function) is applied 1; S2-0009 and S2-0020 (two or three uses, each with a function) are applied 2. No single prompt can match both sets on these calls.
3. **Measured outcomes.** S2-0092 (1 million messages, +108%) and S2-0121 (1 million chats) are applied 1; S2-0056 (1 million interactions) is applied 2.
4. **Products for AI demand.** Applied codes differ for similar supplier or market excerpts: S2-0129 "50% of our bookings this quarter were AI" is applied 0; S2-0123 "AI is about $250 million of sales for us" is applied 1 (conceptual 2); S2-0086 "HPC and AI business" financing is conceptual 2, applied 2.
5. **Question-only excerpt with nonzero codes.** S2-0036 consists only of an analyst question ("Do you see any competitive pressures from the adoption of AI …?"), coded conceptual 0, critical 1, governance 1.
6. **"AI" meaning avian influenza.** S3-0023 is analyst questions about avian-influenza outbreaks ("AI outbreaks", "vaccination … against AI"), coded 0 throughout, which is right. Worth checking that no TEST or 143-set call is coded as artificial intelligence on such text.
7. **Near-identical excerpts.** A TF-IDF check (cosine > 0.35) found one pair per set. S2-0030 and S2-0041 (Samsung Electro-Mechanics, same call) are coded the same. TR065 and TR115 (agentic AI on ERP) are coded 2/1/2/0 and 1/1/1/0.
